Investigate why a sign-in failed (AADSTS-annotated), tenant-wide failure trends and password-spray suspects, who changed what in the directory, and whether MFA is registered for an account, plus a one-shot daily_brief. Read-only — no write calls anywhere in this package. Needs either app-only credentials (ENTRAADM_TENANT_ID/_CLIENT_ID/_CLIENT_SECRET) or an existing `az login` session already present on the machine; the plugin cannot create or supply those for you. ([shigechika/entraadm-mcp](https://github.com/shigechika/entraadm-mcp/tree/HEAD/plugin))
- **entrepreneurship-theory-and-practice-skills**: Twelve-skill bundle covering the Entrepreneurship Theory and Practice manuscript lifecycle. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Entrepreneurship-Theory-and-Practice-Skills))
- **entrinsik-org-claude-plugins**: Claude Code skills and utilities for working with Informer platform and analytics. ([entrinsik-org/claude-plugins](https://github.com/entrinsik-org/claude-plugins))
- **entrix**: Harness Engineering plugin exposing executable fitness functions for architecture quality, change-aware validation, and MCP-powered review context. ([phodal/entrix](https://github.com/phodal/entrix))
- **entroly**: Evidence operations, auditable context selection, and exact recovery for AI coding agents. ([juyterman1000/entroly](https://github.com/juyterman1000/entroly))
- **entropia-maintenance**: Entropia'ya ozgu ajanlar, adjudicated invariant skill'leri ve uretilmis dosyalari koruyan hook'lar. ([alimirbagirzade/Entropia](https://github.com/alimirbagirzade/Entropia/tree/HEAD/plugins/entropia-maintenance))
- **entropy**: Inject real randomness into creative work via seed strings. ([benjaminjackson/entropy-skills](https://github.com/benjaminjackson/entropy-skills))
- **entry**: Create chronologically organized documentation entries ([benthomasson/agentic-mind](https://github.com/benthomasson/agentic-mind/tree/HEAD/plugins/entry))
- **entry-point-analyzer**: Analyzes smart contract codebases to identify state-changing entry points for security auditing. Detects externally callable functions that modify state, categorizes them by access level, and generates structured audit reports. ([trailofbits/skills](https://github.com/trailofbits/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/entry-point-analyzer))
- **entrydesk**: Agent skills for EntryDesk — chat with AI models and agents, schedule agents, and call connected SaaS tools (Slack, GitHub, Google Drive, …) from the terminal. ([gntc-labs/skills](https://github.com/gntc-labs/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/entrydesk))
- **entrypoint-agents**: Entrypoint agent definition for structuring Claude Code sessions ([karashiiro/karashiiro-marketplace](https://github.com/karashiiro/karashiiro-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/entrypoint-agents))
- **entur-linje**: Entur Linje design system: @entur/* React components, design tokens, brand identity, WCAG/universell utforming compliance, and package upgrade migrations. Sourced directly from entur/design-system so there is no vendored copy to drift. ([entur/design-system](https://github.com/entur/design-system/tree/HEAD/skills))
- **entur-uniformen**: Uniformen: Entur's shared header and footer for B2B and partner-facing portals. Integrating @entur/uniformen server-side and language contracts, and the CSP the rendered chrome needs. Sourced directly from entur/uniformen so there is no vendored copy to drift. ([entur/uniformen](https://github.com/entur/uniformen/tree/HEAD/skills))
- **entwurf**: Turn a project into buildable specifications, one expert role at a time. Guided interviews for the three people a project needs: the product owner (goals, scope, vocabulary, then feature specifications), the technical architect (constraints, shared domain model, contracts, then the architecture baseline), and the experience lead (conditions of use, states, components, content). Output is a standard GitHub Spec Kit specs/ folder ready to hand to a development team, or to Fabrik. The product-owner path needs no terminal, git, or technical background. ([handarbeit/fabrik](https://github.com/handarbeit/fabrik/tree/HEAD/plugin/entwurf))
- **enuff-is-enuff-unsubscribe**: Find recurring email junk and make it stop with Claude. Scan inbox exports, review a local approval report, then unsubscribe, filter, or delete only after confirmation. ([codecoincognition/enuff-is-enuff-unsubscribe](https://github.com/codecoincognition/enuff-is-enuff-unsubscribe))
- **env**: Claude Code 환경 동기화 — Gist 기반 upload/download로 플러그인, MCP, hooks, settings, ccstatusline을 여러 PC에서 동일 유지 ([Kang-Jacob-GitLB/mccm](https://github.com/Kang-Jacob-GitLB/mccm/tree/HEAD/plugins/env))
